Model before rewriting:

find x: int(-3..-2, 0, 3)
find at_most_two: bool
find b: bool
find c: bool
find zero: bool
find mixed: int(-3..3)
find quotient: int(-3..3)
find remainder: int(0..1)

such that

mixed = product([x,toInt(b);int(1..)]),
(zero) <-> (product([x,toInt(b);int(1..)]) = 0),
product([x,toInt(and([b,x != 0;int(1..)]));int(1..)]) = mixed,
quotient = (x / sum([toInt(b),1;int(1..)])),
remainder = x % sum([toInt(b),1;int(1..)]),
(at_most_two) <-> (sum([toInt(b),toInt(c),toInt(x % 2 != 0),toInt(x != 0);int(1..4)]) <= 2)
